10, 11, 12], f=<_io.TextIOWrapper name=\'/home/user/HyperAgents/exp/runs/source_s4/generate_source_s4/generate_loop.log\' mode=\'a\' encoding=\'UTF-8\'>\n--- task agent ---\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport socket\nimport http.client\nimport time\n\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain. The second permitted model call\n# independently verifies the first candidate against the demonstrations before returning it.\n\n\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\n        self._path = path\n\n    def connect(self):\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\n        s.connect(self._path)\n        self.sock = s\n\n\ndef call_model(prompt):\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\n    last = None\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\n            r = c.getresponse()\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\n            c.close()\n            if r.status == 200:\n                return data.get("text", "")\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\'error\')}"\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\n\n\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\n    return "\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\n\n\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\n    parts = [\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule, check that it reproduces every "\n        "demonstration exactly, and apply it to the test input.",\n        "",\n    ]\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\n        parts.append("")\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\n    parts.append("## Test")\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\n    parts.append("")\n    parts.append(\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\n"\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\n    )\n    return "\\n".join(parts)\n\n\ndef _valid(g):\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\n\n\ndef _parse_block(s):\n    s = s.strip()\n    try:\n        g = json.loads(s)\n        if _valid(g):\n            return g\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\n    rows = []\n    for line in s.splitlines():\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\n        if not line:\n            continue\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\d\\s,]+", line) else None\n        if not toks:\n            return None\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\n\n\ndef parse_grid(text):\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\n    if not text:\n        return None\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\[\\s*\\[[\\d\\s,\\[\\]]*\\]\\s*\\]", text)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    return None\n\n\nclass TaskAgent:\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\n        self.model = model\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\n\n    def log(self, msg):\n        try:\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\n                f.write(msg + "\\n\\n")\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    def solve_once(self, inputs, previous_candidate=None):\n        """Make one model call and parse its grid response, if any."""\n        prompt = build_prompt(inputs)\n        if previous_candidate is not None:\n            prompt += (\n                "\\n\\nA first attempt proposed this candidate test output:\\n"\n                f"{json.dumps(previous_candidate)}\\n"\n                "Do not assume that candidate is correct. Independently infer and re-check the rule "\n                "against every training input-output pair, then compare the rule\'s result with the "\n                "candidate. The first candidate will also be kept as an answer, so use this call to "\n                "maximize the chance of a second exact match: if the examples support a plausible "\n                "different interpretation, return the test grid for that alternative; otherwise "\n                "confirm the original candidate. Do not invent an unsupported alternative. Return "\n                "one grid in the required <answer> tags."\n            )\n        self.log(f"Input: {prompt}")\n        text = call_model(prompt)\n        self.log(f"Output: {text}")\n        return parse_grid(text)\n\n    def forward(self, inputs):\n        """\n        Args:\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\n        """\n        answers = []\n        for attempt in range(2):\n            try:\n                # Use the second call for explicit verification when the first call\n                # yielded a candidate; if not, use it as another independent attempt.\n                previous = answers[0] if attempt == 1 and answers else None\n                g = self.solve_once(inputs, previous_candidate=previous)\n            except Exception as e:\n                self.log(f"Error: {e}")\n                g = None\n            if g is not None:\n                answers.append(g)\n        return answers, []\n--- generator structure ---\n29:    get_domain_eval_subset,\n31:    get_domain_stagedeval_samples,\n35:    get_patch_files,\n45:    process_meta_patch_files,\n49:def run_harness_polyglot(root_dir, output_dir, genid, skip_staged_eval=False, num_samples=-1):\n54:    eval_output_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"gen_{genid}", "polyglot_eval")\n56:    model_name_or_path = "eval_run"\n57:    patch_files = get_patch_files(output_dir, genid)\n58:    run_next_eval = True\n60:    # Small sample size evaluation for staged eval\n61:    if not skip_staged_eval:\n68:            model_patch_paths=patch_files,\n69:            num_evals=1,\n70:            num_evals_parallel=1,\n71:            pred_dname=eval_output_dir,\n72:            output_dir=eval_output_dir,\n75:        report_polyglot(output_dir=eval_output_dir, run_keyword=model_name_or_path, expected_num_tasks=len(test_task_list))\n76:        stagedeval_score = get_score("polyglot", output_dir, genid)\n77:        run_next_eval = stagedeval_score is not None and stagedeval_score >= test_more_threshold\n79:    # A grid is a rectangular list of rows of integers 0-9 (at most 30x30).\n\n## Task agent interface\n`task_agent.py` must define `TaskAgent(model=..., chat_history_file=...)` with\n`forward(inputs) -> (prediction, msg_history)`, called once per test input with\n`inputs = {"domain": "arc", "train": [...], "test_input": grid}`.\n`prediction` is a list of up to 2 candidate output grids (a single grid is also accepted).\n\n## Scoring\nExact match of the full output grid. eval_produced_agent(\\n363:def copy_prev_eval_to_container(\\n427:def generate(\\n719:def generate_loop(\\n\\n--- agent files ---\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport socket\\nimport http.client\\nimport time\\n\\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\\n\\n\\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\\n        self._path = path\\n\\n    def connect(self):\\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\\n        s.connect(self._path)\\n        self.sock = s\\n\\n\\ndef call_model(prompt):\\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\\n    last = None\\n    for attempt in range(3):\\n        try:\\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\\n            r = c.getresponse()\\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\\n            c.close()\\n            if r.status == 200:\\n                return data.get("text", "")\\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\\\'error\\\')}"\\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\\n                break\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\\n\\n\\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\\n    return "\\\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\\n    parts = [\\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input.",\\n        "",\\n    ]\\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\\n        parts.append("")\\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\\n    parts.append("## Test")\\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\\n    parts.append("")\\n    parts.append(\\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\\\n"\\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\\n    )\\n    return "\\\\n".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef _valid(g):\\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_block(s):\\n    s = s.strip()\\n    try:\\n        g = json.loads(s)\\n        if _valid(g):\\n            return g\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\\n    rows = []\\n    for line in s.splitlines():\\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\\n        if not line:\\n            continue\\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\\\d\\\\s,]+", line) else None\\n        if not toks:\\n            return None\\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\\n\\n\\ndef parse_grid(text):\\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\\n    if not text:\\n        return None\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\\\[\\\\s*\\\\[[\\\\d\\\\s,\\\\[\\\\]]*\\\\]\\\\s*\\\\]", text)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    return None\\n\\n\\nclass TaskAgent:\\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\\n        self.model = model\\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\\n\\n    def log(self, msg):\\n        try:\\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\\n                f.write(msg + "\\\\n\\\\n")\\n        except Exception:\\n            pass\\n\\n    def solve_once(self, inputs):\\n        """One direct model response -> one parsed grid (or None)."""\\n        prompt = build_prompt(inputs)\\n        self.log(f"Input: {prompt}")\\n        text = call_model(prompt)\\n        self.log(f"Output: {text}")\\n        return parse_grid(text)\\n\\n    def forward(self, inputs):\\n        """\\n        Args:\\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\\n        Returns:\\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\\n        """\\n        answers = []\\n        for _ in range(2):\\n            try:\\n                g = self.solve_once(inputs)\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                self.log(f"Error: {e}")\\n                g = None\\n            if g is not None:\\n                answers.append(g)\\n        return answers, []\\n# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.\\n\\nfrom agent.base_agent import AgentSystem\\nfrom agent.llm_withtools import chat_with_agent\\n\\nclass number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future. new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(instruction, model=self.model, msg_history=[], logging=self.log, tools_available=\\\'all\\\')"\\n    }\\n    </json>\'\nOutput: \'<json>\\n{\\n  "tool_name": output_dir, genid, skip_staged_eval=False, num_samples=-1):\\n    # NOTE: the harness for polyglot is different because each task instance needs a docker container\\n    from domains.polyglot.harness import harness as harness_polyglot\\n    from domains.polyglot.report import report as report_polyglot\\n\\n    eval_output_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"gen_{genid}", "polyglot_eval")\\n    test_more_threshold = 0.4  # NOTE: same setting as that in DGM\\n    model_name_or_path = "eval_run"\\n    patch_files = get_patch_files(output_dir, genid)\\n    run_next_eval = True\\n\\n    # Small sample size evaluation for staged eval\\n    if not skip_staged_eval:\\n        test_task_list = load_json_file("./domains/polyglot/subsets/small.json")\\n        dnames = harness_polyglot(\\n            test_task_list=test_task_list,\\n            num_samples=-1,\\n            max_workers=10,\\n            model_name_or_path=model_name_or_path,\\n            model_patch_paths=patch_files,\\n            num_evals=1,\\n            num_evals_parallel=1,\\n            pred_dname=eval_output_dir,\\n            output_dir=eval_output_dir,\\n            root_dir=root_dir,\\n        )\\n        report_polyglot(output_dir=eval_output_dir, run_keyword=model_name_or_path, expected_num_tasks=len(test_task_list))\\n        stagedeval_score = get_score("polyglot", output_dir, genid)\\n        run_next_eval = stagedeval_score is not None and stagedeval_score >= test_more_threshold\\n\\n    # Check if additional evaluation should be run\\n    if run_next_eval:\\n        test_task_list_more = load_json_file("./domains/polyglot/subsets/medium.json")\\n        dnames = harness_polyglot(\\n            test_task_list=test_task_list + test_task_list_more,\\n            num_samples=num_samples,\\n            max_workers=10,\\n            model_name_or_path=model_name_or_path,\\n            model_patch_paths=patch_files,\\n            num_evals=1,\\n            num_evals_parallel=1,\\n            pred_dname=eval_output_dir,\\n            output_dir=eval_output_dir,\\n            root_dir=root_dir,\\n        )\\n        report_polyglot(output_dir=eval_output_dir, run_keyword=model_name_or_path, expected_num_tasks=len(test_task_list + test_task_list_more))\\n\\n    # Update metadata\\n    update_node_metadata(output_dir, genid, {"run_full_eval": run_next_eval})\\n\\ndef select_next_parent_container(\\n    docker_client,\\n    domains,\\n    generate_output_dir,\\n    archive,\\n    root_dir="./",\\n    root_commit="HEAD",\\n    max_attempts=10,\\n):\\n    # Setup logger\\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(generate_output_dir, "select_next_parent.log"))\\n\\n    # Get the latest node that can select parent\\n    latest_node = get_latest_can_select_parent(archive, generate_output_dir)\\n    safe_log(f"select_next_parent_container: latest_node={latest_node}")\\n    prev_patch_files = get_patch_files(generate_output_dir, latest_node)\\n\\n    # Create and start the Docker container\\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\\n    run_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")\\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-nextparent-container-{run_id}"\\n    container = build_container(docker_client, root_dir, image_name, container_name, verbose=False)\\n    container.start()\\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\\n\\n    try:\\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files, verbose=False)\\n\\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\\n        container_generate_output_dir = os.path.join(\\n            container_output_folder, generate_output_dir.split(os.sep)[-1]\\n        )\\n        copy_to_container(\\n            container,\\n            source_path=generate_output_dir,\\n            dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\\n            verbose=False,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Get next parent in container\\n        command = [\\n            "timeout",\\n            "3600",  # 1h timeout\\n            "python",\\n            "-m",\\n            "utils.run_select_next_parent",\\n            "--domains",\\n            *domains,\\n            "--generate_output_dir",\\n            container_generate_output_dir,\\n        ]\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=True)\\n\\n        # Get next parent outputs\\n        container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\\\n")\\n        next_parent_genid = container_output_strings[-1]\\n        next_parent_genid = int(next_parent_genid) if not is_starting_node(next_parent_genid) else next_parent_genid\\n\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        safe_log(f"Error in select_next_parent_container: {e}")\\n        update_node_metadata(generate_output_dir, latest_node, {"can_select_next_parent": False})\\n        next_parent_genid = None\\n\\n    # Even on errors or KeyboardInterrupt\\n    finally:\\n        # Reset to the root commit\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\\n            cmd=["git", "reset", "--hard", root_commit], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\\n        )\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=False)\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\\n            cmd=["git", "clean", "-fd"], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\\n        )\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=False)\\n\\n        # Cleanup container\\n        cleanup_container(container, verbose=False)\\n\\n    # Try again if no parent is selected\\n    if next_parent_genid is None:\\n        if max_attempts > 0:\\n            next_parent_genid = select_next_parent_container(\\n                docker_client,\\n                domains,\\n                generate_output_dir,\\n                archive,\\n                root_dir,\\n                root_commit,\\n                max_attempts=max_attempts - 1,\\n            )\\n        else:\\n            # In case of infinite recursive, but it should not happen\\n            raise Exception("Max attempts reached in select_next_parent_container")\\n\\n    return next_parent_genid\\n\\ndef repo_path (str): The path to the repository.\\n            eval_path (str): The path to previously generated agents and their evaluation results.\\n            iterations_left (int, optional): The The second permitted model call\\n# independently verifies the first candidate against the demonstrations before returning it.\\n\\n\\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\\n        self._path = path\\n\\n    def connect(self):\\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\\n        s.connect(self._path)\\n        self.sock = s\\n\\n\\ndef call_model(prompt):\\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\\n    last = None\\n    for attempt in range(3):\\n        try:\\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\\n            r = c.getresponse()\\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\\n            c.close()\\n            if r.status == 200:\\n                return data.get("text", "")\\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\\\'error\\\')}"\\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\\n                break\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\\n\\n\\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\\n    return "\\\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\\n    parts = [\\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule, check that it reproduces every "\\n        "demonstration exactly, and apply it to the test input.",\\n        "",\\n    ]\\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\\n        parts.append("")\\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\\n    parts.append("## Test")\\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\\n    parts.append("")\\n    parts.append(\\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\\\n"\\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\\n    )\\n    return "\\\\n".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef _valid(g):\\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_block(s):\\n    s = s.strip()\\n    try:\\n        g = json.loads(s)\\n        if _valid(g):\\n            return g\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\\n    rows = []\\n    for line in s.splitlines():\\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\\n        if not line:\\n            continue\\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\\\d\\\\s,]+", line) else None\\n        if not toks:\\n            return None\\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\\n\\n\\ndef parse_grid(text):\\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\\n    if not text:\\n        return None\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\\\[\\\\s*\\\\[[\\\\d\\\\s,\\\\[\\\\]]*\\\\]\\\\s*\\\\]", text)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    return None\\n\\n\\nclass TaskAgent:\\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\\n        self.model = model\\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\\n\\n    def log(self, msg):\\n        try:\\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\\n                f.write(msg + "\\\\n\\\\n")\\n        except Exception:\\n            pass\\n\\n    def solve_once(self, inputs, previous_candidate=None):\\n        """Make one model call and parse its grid response, if any."""\\n        prompt = build_prompt(inputs)\\n        if previous_candidate is not None:\\n            prompt += (\\n                "\\\\n\\\\nA first attempt proposed this candidate test output:\\\\n"\\n                f"{json.dumps(previous_candidate)}\\\\n"\\n                "Do not assume that candidate is correct. Independently infer and re-check the rule "\\n                "against every training input-output pair, then compare the rule\\\'s result with the "\\n                "candidate. The first candidate will also be kept as an answer, so use this call to "\\n                "maximize the chance of a second exact match: if the examples support a plausible "\\n                "different interpretation, return the test grid for that alternative; otherwise "\\n                "confirm the original candidate. Do not invent an unsupported alternative. Return "\\n                "one grid in the required <answer> tags."\\n            )\\n        self.log(f"Input: {prompt}")\\n        text = call_model(prompt)\\n        self.log(f"Output: {text}")\\n        return parse_grid(text)\\n\\n    def forward(self, inputs):\\n        """\\n        Args:\\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\\n        Returns:\\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\\n        """\\n        answers = []\\n        for attempt in range(2):\\n            try:\\n                # Use the second call for explicit verification when the first call\\n                # yielded a candidate; if not, use it as another independent attempt.\\n                previous = answers[0] if attempt == 1 and answers else None\\n                g = self.solve_once(inputs, previous_candidate=previous)\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                self.log(f"Error: {e}")\\n                g = None\\n            if g is not None:\\n                answers.append(g)\\n        return answers, []\\n--- gl_utils relevant A grid is a rectangular list of rows of integers 0-9 (at most 30x30).\\n\\n## Task agent interface\\n`task_agent.py` must define `TaskAgent(model=..., chat_history_file=...)` with\\n`forward(inputs) -> (prediction, msg_history)`, called once per test input with\\n`inputs = {"domain": "arc", "train": [...], "test_input": grid}`.\\n`prediction` is a list of up to 2 candidate output grids (a single grid is also accepted).\\n\\n## Scoring\\nExact match of the full output grid. Two answers count per test input: a test input is solved if\\neither of the first two returned grids equals the true output. A task\\\'s score is the share of its\\ntest inputs solved; a set\\\'s score (`overall_accuracy`) is the mean over tasks. Scoring happens\\noutside the agent\\\'s environment; true test outputs are never available to agents.\\n\\n## Budget and environment (enforced outside the agent\\\'s code)\\n* At most 2 model calls per test input (further calls are refused) and 2 answers per test input.\\n  Model and reasoning effort are fixed by the gateway; call it as in `task_agent.py` / `agent/llm.py`.\\n* No network. Each test input runs in a fresh process whose working directory `/tmp/task` holds only\\n  that task\\\'s example pairs and the test input (`task.json`).\\n* Model-written code can be executed with `from arc_sandbox import run_python` (no network, fresh\\n  temp dir with `task.json`, 30 seconds wall clock per test input in total).\\n* Visible tasks of the current set (example pairs + test inputs, no test outputs) are in\\n  `domains/arc/data/tasks.json`. Evaluation results of earlier generations (per-task scores, wrong\\n  answers, chat logs of unsolved test inputs) are in the generation folders of the evals folder.\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\nfrom agent.llm import OPENAI_MODEL\\nfrom utils.thread_logger import ThreadLoggerManager\\n\\n\\nclass AgentSystem(ABC):\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        model=OPENAI_MODEL,\\n        chat_history_file=\\\'./outputs/chat_history.md\\\',\\n    ):\\n        self.model = model\\n\\n        # Initialize logger and store it in thread-local storage\\n        self.logger_manager = ThreadLoggerManager(log_file=chat_history_file)\\n        self.log = self.logger_manager.log\\n\\n        # Clear the log file\\n        with open(chat_history_file, \\\'w\\\') as f:\\n            f.write(\\\'\\\')\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def forward(self, *args, **kwargs):\\n        pass\\nimport re\\nimport json\\n\\nfrom agent.llm import get_response_from_llm\\nfrom agent.tools import load_tools\\n\\ndef get_tooluse_prompt(tool_infos=[]):\\n    """\\n    Get the prompt for using the available tools.\\n    """\\n    # If no tools are available, return an empty string\\n    if not tool_infos or len(tool_infos) == 0:\\n        return ""\\n    # Create the prompt\\n    tools_available = [str(tool_info) for tool_info in tool_infos]\\n    tools_available = \\\'\\\\n\\\\n\\\'.join(tools_available) if tools_available else \\\'None\\\'\\n    tooluse_prompt = """Here are the available tools:\\n```\\n{tools_available}\\n```\\n\\nUse only one tool (if needed) in this format:\\n<json>\\n{{\\n    "tool_name": ...,\\n    "tool_input": ...\\n}}\\n</json>\\n\\nONLY USE ONE TOOL PER RESPONSE, AND STRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\\n""".format(tools_available=tools_available)\\n    return tooluse_prompt.strip()\\n\\ndef should_retry_tool_use(response, tool_uses=None):\\n    """\\n    Check if the response attempts to use a tool,\\n    but ran out of output context.\\n    """\\n    # If there are tool uses, we don\\\'t need to check for retry\\n    if tool_uses is not None and len(tool_uses) > 0:\\n        return False\\n\\n    # Find positions of the markers\\n    json_pos = response.find("<json>")\\n    tool_name_pos = response.find("tool_name")\\n    tool_input_pos = response.find("tool_input")\\n\\n    # Check ordering and length condition\\n    if (\\n        json_pos != -1\\n        and tool_name_pos != -1\\n        and tool_input_pos != -1\\n        and json_pos < tool_name_pos < tool_input_pos\\n        and len(response) >= 2000\\n    ):\\n        return True\\n\\n    # No retry\\n    return False\\n\\ndef check_for_tool_uses(response):\\n    """\\n    Checks if the response contains one or more tool calls in json code blocks.\\n    Returns a list of tool use dictionaries.\\n    """\\n    pattern = r\\\'<json>\\\\s*(\\\\{.*?\\\\})\\\\s*</json>\\\'\\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, response, re.DOTALL)\\n    tool_uses = []\\n\\n    for match in matches:\\n        try:\\n            tool_use = json.loads(match)\\n            if \\\'tool_name\\\' not in tool_use or \\\'tool_input\\\' not in tool_use:\\n                continue  # Skip invalid tool use\\n            tool_uses.append(tool_use)\\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n            continue  # Skip malformed JSON blocks\\n\\n    # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] also accept a bare JSON tool call without <json> tags\\n    if not tool_uses:\\n        dec = json.JSONDecoder()\\n        i = response.find(\\\'{\\\')\\n        while i != -1:\\n            try:\\n                obj, end = dec.raw_decode(response, i)\\n                if isinstance(obj, dict) and \\\'tool_name\\\' in obj and \\\'tool_input\\\' in obj:\\n                    tool_uses.append(obj)\\n                    break\\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n                pass\\n            i = response.find(\\\'{\\\', i + 1)\\n\\n    return tool_uses if tool_uses else None\\n\\ndef process_tool_call(tools_dict, tool_name, tool_input):\\n    try:\\n        if tool_name in tools_dict:\\n            return tools_dict[tool_name][\\\'function\\\'](**tool_input)\\n        else:\\n            return f"Error: Tool \\\'{tool_name}\\\' not found"\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        return f"Error executing tool \\\'{tool_name}\\\': {str(e)}"\\n\\ndef chat_with_agent(\\n    msg,\\n    model="claude-4-sonnet-genai",\\n    msg_history=None,\\n    logging=print,\\n    tools_available=[],  # Empty list means no tools, \\\'all\\\' means all tools\\n    multiple_tool_calls=False,  # Whether to allow multiple tool calls in a single response\\n    max_tool_calls=40,  # Maximum number of tool calls allowed in a single response, -1 for unlimited\\n):\\n    get_response_fn = get_response_from_llm\\n    # Construct message\\n    if msg_history is None:\\n        msg_history = []\\n    new_msg_history = msg_history\\n\\n    try:\\n        # Load all tools\\n        all_tools = load_tools(logging=logging, names=tools_available)\\n        tools_dict = {tool[\\\'info\\\'][\\\'name\\\']: tool for tool in all_tools}\\n        system_msg = f"{get_tooluse_prompt([tool[\\\'info\\\'] for tool in all_tools])}\\\\n\\\\n"\\n        num_tool_calls = 0\\n\\n        # Call API\\n        logging(f"Input: {repr(msg)}")\\n        response, new_msg_history, info = get_response_fn(\\n            msg=system_msg + msg,\\n            model=model,\\n            msg_history=new_msg_history,\\n        )\\n        logging(f"Output: {repr(response)}")\\n        # logging(f"Info: {repr(info)}")\\n\\n        # Tool use\\n        tool_uses = check_for_tool_uses(response)\\n        retry_tool_use = should_retry_tool_use(response, tool_uses)\\n        num_nudges = 0\\n        while True:\\n            # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna sometimes ends a turn after only\\n            # announcing what it will do; nudge (at most 2 times in a row) before treating it as finished.\\n            if not (tool_uses or retry_tool_use):\\n                if num_nudges >= 2 or \\\'DONE\\\' in response or (max_tool_calls > 0 and num_tool_calls >= max_tool_calls):\\n                    break\\n                num_nudges += 1\\n                nudge = ("No tool call was found in your last response. Please try again.")\\n                tool_msgs.append("Error: Output context exceeded. Please try again.")\\n\\n            # Get tool response\\n            response, new_msg_history, info = get_response_fn(\\n                msg=system_msg + \\\'\\\\n\\\\n\\\'.join(tool_msgs),\\n                model=model,\\n                msg_history=new_msg_history,\\n            )\\n            logging(f"Output: {repr(response)}")\\n            # logging(f"Info: {repr(info)}")\\n\\n
